## 2026-07-18 — CMS rework: categorized shell, live storefront preview, speed affordances, Gamification section

- **Admin shell + IA rework.** The drawer-only nav became a **persistent categorized sidebar** (≥1024px, collapsible to an icon rail with the preference persisted in `anvl.adminSidebar.v1`; drawer below `lg`). The 13 surfaces group into Dashboard · Design · Content · Commerce · Passports · Gamification · Media · Settings — nav-only grouping, every `/admin/*` URL unchanged. `adminNav.ts` is the single IA source (sidebar, topbar breadcrumbs — the stale `sectionLabels` map referencing dead routes is gone — and dashboard cards all derive from it). New chrome: `AdminShell` (AdminLayout keeps its API and delegates), per-item Phosphor icons, a topbar **Preview** toggle. Cross-navigation landed: `/admin/assets?page=&slot=&q=` scopes the slot panel + highlights a slot + seeds the library search; `/admin/passports?tab=content&product=` opens that product's wizard; the PDP editor links to its product's passport content.
- **Live storefront preview (net-new).** The Preview panel embeds the REAL storefront in a same-origin iframe (`?anvl-cms-preview=1`) and pushes the editors' **unsaved** in-memory working copies over a Zod-validated postMessage bridge (`hello`/`draft`/`focus` → `ready`/`located`, v1). Storefront side (`src/features/cms/preview/`) is SSR-safe and visitor-safe: null until post-mount, activates only inside an iframe on the same origin after the handshake. Draft slices override published data in `SiteThemeProvider` (instant CSS-var repaint), the home landing (content + re-resolved asset slots), About, `/shop` config, PDP content, and Coming Soon; previewing a non-home route bypasses the Coming Soon gate inside the iframe only. Device switcher (desktop 1280 — genuinely triggers the Oath cinematic gate — tablet 768, mobile 390, scale-fitted); closing unmounts the iframe (no idle WebGL). **Locate** buttons in the editors scroll/ring the exact element in the preview (`data-anvl-preview-target`; Oath scenes resolve via their existing `data-scene` contract; About hero + every orb instrumented). Save = publish is untouched — the preview covers the pre-save gap that previously had no coverage at all.
- **Speed affordances.** Generic **`AdminWizard`** extracted from the 642-line `PassportContentWizard` (now ~145 lines + step bodies in `passportWizardSteps.tsx` — MAINT-01 progress); **native HTML5 drag-reorder** via a new `useSortableList` hook (no dependency; keyboard up/down fallback everywhere) on About orbs, the Oath product showcase, story acts (renumbering upserts), and gamification challenges; **media drag-to-slot** — library cards drag onto any `MediaLibrarySlotField` or slot-panel row; the upload-naming modal's slot select gained a **"Custom name…"** option (kebab-forced) for every registry context; the dashboard gained a **drop-setup checklist** with live completion ticks. (Descoped knowingly: wizardizing batch generation — already a single step — and story chapter creation, which fights the local-draft wizard model with relational children.)
- **Gamification section (net-new) + Supabase tables.** `/admin/gamification` edits the Armory's rules in four tabs — Ranks (fixed 4 keys; copy, emblem override via the media library, per-level AND-combined thresholds), Challenges (drag-reorder, declarative metric ∈ {registrations, total_wears, max_wears, feat_count, full_drops, honor_pinned} + target, active toggle, create/delete), Forge XP (4 constants + level-curve factor with a live preview table), Badges (metric+target milestones). Migration `20260718120000_gamification_rules.sql` creates `gamification_settings/ranks/rank_levels/challenges/badges` (RLS: public SELECT — rules are public data — editor/admin write, story-tables pattern) and **seeds the exact historical hardcoded values** (applied to the live project). `passport/lib/{ranks,challenges,forgeXp}.ts` became rules-taking resolvers with `DEFAULT_GAMIFICATION_RULES` (== seed) as default parameters; a matrix test proves the declarative rank derivation reproduces the old ladder for every (claims × fullDrops). Storefront reads via `useGamificationRules` (anon fetch over `supabaseRest`, React Query, defaults as placeholder — SSR projection untouched); threaded through ArmoryPanel, RankLadderModal, RankBadge, PublicArmoryView, PassportShareSection.
- **Cleanup.** Deleted the deprecated `/admin-preview` redirect route and every `VITE_ADMIN_PREVIEW_ENABLED` reference (README, CLAUDE.md, backend-guidelines, deployment, project-map); MAINT-03 confirmed resolved (the `ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS` registry covers `anvl.landingContent.v1`, and the new sidebar preference joined it); docs updated (cms-architecture: shell/IA + preview protocol + gamification; backend-guidelines: policy model; CLAUDE.md: routes/folders/tables).

## 2026-07-18 — Round 12b: the altar strike as an anime cut, forging modals everywhere, armory index

- **Altar strike root-caused and rebuilt as an anime cut.** Root cause of "no particles": the strike modal's backdrop used a static `backdrop-blur-md` and was "hidden" by animating opacity — but `backdrop-filter` keeps blurring the canvas even at opacity 0 (Chromium), so from the moment the modal mounted (0.2s after impact) the entire ember flight was smeared away. The blur is now GSAP-driven (`backdropFilter: blur(0→10px)`) alongside the opacity. On top of the fix, the strike gained anime staging: a white-hot **impact flash** + radial **speed-line ring** (DOM overlays, snap in ~60–90ms and burn off), a **hit-stop** (the hammer stays buried ~0.14s before the recoil), **molten shard debris** (22 additive tumbling tetrahedra that arc under gravity and burn out as the gather starts), the hanging ember cloud slowly **revolving** around the anvil, and the swarm now **spirals in** to form the modal plate (each ember unwinds its own arc as its radius collapses) instead of lerping straight. The panel materializes with a perspective tilt (`rotateX` 7°→0) and an **ignition edge flash** in the struck orb's color. Pool raised to 2 400; ring/shard/ember colors stay theme tokens.
- **Every shared modal now forges from embers** (`ModalForgeEffect` + `.anvl-modal-forge`): on open, ~520 theme-ramp embers converge from a scattered ring onto the panel's rectangle (62% border trace, the rest dusting the face), burn hot cores as they land, and dissolve as the panel materializes (0.4s reveal delayed 0.45s); the backdrop fades instead of snapping. Deliberately **canvas-2D** — Modal sits in the shared chunk both admin and storefront load, so no `vendor-three` enters that path. Reduced motion skips the ceremony entirely (panel + backdrop instant).
- **Story hero ledger fixed**: the volumes/books/acts values were bone-on-bone against the ghost SAGA inscription — they now wear the champagne foil gradient on a 72%-bg scrim rail.
- **Guest armory round 2**: record numbers sit higher on the identity card (top-aligned on desktop); Hall of Honor pieces lost the ghost numeral and the plinth (the pooled light stays — "Honor I/II/III" lives in the caption only); the TCG card back flips back on a click anywhere (sr-only button keeps the keyboard/AT path), the ANVL watermark nudged up-left, the flip icon became a **shop link** (`/shop/$slug`), and "Show all" next to Latest feats opens a **full product-detail modal** — catalog facts (drop, category, price, cut, fabric, weight from a new loader-supplied `meta` map), the piece's service record, and every feat. Above the collection, a new **filter bar**: search + category + drop selects whose options grow dynamically from the owner's actual pieces.

## 2026-07-18 — Round 12: story chrome as a war chronicle, TCG guest armory, standard-ember altar burst

- **Story page chrome redesigned (books/shelf/overlay untouched)**: the centred candlelit "Athenaeum" hero became an asymmetric **war-chronicle frontispiece** — a colossal outlined "SAGA" ghost inscription bleeding off the right edge, the title block ranged hard-left with a border-rule description, and a **ledger rail** (volumes / books / acts, zero-padded Anton numerals) on a champagne hairline. The per-drop `VolumePlaque` boxes are gone; each drop now opens with a **chronicle spine divider** (outlined colossal roman numeral + "Volume N · Drop record" + a heat rule running toward the shelf). The whole page sits in one continuous `ChronicleAtmosphere` (cold vault falling to an ember-lit floor, margin shelf-column hairlines masked off centre, deterministic drifting sparks, grain) instead of per-section backgrounds, and the colophon became a corner-ticked **closing plate**. `StoryShelf`/`StoryShelf3D`/`ChapterShelf`/`ChapterCover`/`ChapterBook` untouched.
- **Guest armory (`/armory/$handle`) overhauled**: one bevel-cut **identity card** leads — rank emblem on a lit disc, name, rank title with I/II/III level pips, fun-stat chips (days in service, most-worn piece, honors held), and the record numbers (pieces / wears / feats) on a hairline rail, with an "ARMORY" ghost inscription. The **Hall of Honor** stands on lit pedestals (outlined ghost numeral behind each piece, pooled glow + trapezoid plinth beneath, hover lift). The collection deals every piece as a **TCG-style flip card** (`ArmoryTcgCard`, aspect 5:7, octagonal clip, temper-tiered frame heat by wear count — Fresh steel / Tempered / Battle-forged / Relic): front is full-art with a name plate, fit line, wear/feat counts and a hover holo sweep; clicking flips (3D preserve-3d, reduced-motion snaps, `inert` + `aria-hidden` face management) to the **service record** back — wears, days in service, fit, the latest three feats, and an "All feats" modal when more exist. Loose feats keep the "More feats" ledger. All entrances via `RevealOnScroll`; new flip test in `publicArmory.test.tsx`.
- **About altar burst joined the site ember standard**: the strike's burst particles dropped their flat per-orb-colored dots for the **particle-forge heat ramp** used by the passport console and Coming Soon anvil — `uColdColor/uEmberColor/uHotColor` read from theme tokens (`--color-surface-elevated`→`--color-heading` lerp, `--color-highlight`, `--color-highlight-bright`), hot-core sprite with per-seed twinkle, heat life (white-hot off the strike → cooling drift → re-heating as each ember lands on the forming plate), pool raised to 2000. The disperse → drift → form-the-modal → dissolve choreography is unchanged; the shockwave ring alone keeps the struck orb's color as its identity.

## 2026-07-17 — Round 11c: altar strike re-sequenced so the ember-forming reads

- **The particle-forming was invisible — two timing bugs fixed.** (1) The modal panel faded in 0.6s after mounting, *while* the gather was still running, so the panel just appeared normally and masked the whole effect. (2) The gather (`formT`) started the instant the modal measured itself (~0.15s after impact), while the disperse (`burstT`) was only ~15% done — the embers never visibly spread before snapping to the rectangle. The strike is now strictly sequential: orb shatters into embers (`explodeT` 0.28s) → embers fly out and hold/drift (`burstT` 0.6s + a 0.55s drift hold) → the swarm gathers into the plate (`formT` 0.85s) → the panel materializes as they dissolve (`formFade` 0.5s, panel reveal delayed to 1.5s after mount to land on the dissolve). The dark modal backdrop is also held until the swarm has gathered (delay 1.35s) — raised earlier it would blur/dim the very embers forming the plate, since it sits over the WebGL canvas.

## 2026-07-17 — Round 11b: the altar's particle-formed modal, regenerated forge assets, story re-forged

- **The palantír orb now explodes into the site's ember pool, and that swarm FORMS the modal**: the strike "burst" became the standard disperse-and-rearrange particle system (`aFrom → aTo` + per-seed stagger, 1400 embers). Each ember is born on the seated orb's own sphere (`aFrom` on the shell at `ORB_RADIUS·ORB_SEAT_SCALE`), so as the palantír shader dissolves (`explodeT`) the pool takes its matter over 1:1 — the stone visibly shatters into particles rather than emitting a separate spark spray. `state.burstT` scatters them outward along their surface normals into a drifting cloud; the modal mounts hidden, measures its rect (`onMeasure` → NDC → unprojected onto the orb-seat plane), and `state.formT` staggers the swarm back in to draw the panel's rectangle (60% perimeter, 40% interior fill); `state.formFade` dissolves them as the real panel materializes inside the formed frame. New `AltarState` fields: `formT`, `formFade`, `formSeq`, `modalNdc`.
- **Hammer, anvil, and forge backdrop regenerated (Higgsfield)** to represent ANVL: a monumental obsidian forge hall with an empty centre aisle and champagne ember glow (`public/about/forge-backdrop.webp`), a hand-hammered obsidian anvil with a bronze waist band, and a vertical black sledge with a champagne collar and leather grip — both lifted to textured GLBs (`image_to_3d`, `should_texture`) replacing the bundled `anvil.glb`/`hammer.glb`. The hammer mount offset is now CONSTRUCTED (`-ARM + HAMMER_SIZE/2` puts the striking face exactly at the seat) instead of tuned to the old mesh. **Note:** CMS asset-slot assignments override these bundled defaults — clear the About slots in `/admin/assets` to see them.
- **Story pages re-forged to the site ideology (books untouched)**: every hardcoded gilded hex (`#c8a45a` family) in the hero/saga chrome replaced with theme tokens (the foil headline gradient now derives from `--color-highlight*`, so the hall re-tempers with every CMS palette), and the volume plaques became forged plates (bevel-cut corners, champagne heat hairline, drop-shadow) — the same plate language as the toasts and the Hall of Honor. The 3D bookshelf, book covers, and the book overlay are untouched.

## 2026-07-17 — Feedback round 11: Phosphor final, forged Switch, armory-first sharing

- **Phosphor is final**: `lucide-react` removed from `package.json`; the `@/shared/icons` seam became `index.tsx` and inlines the one icon Phosphor lacks (`Anvil`, lucide path, ISC). Duotone weight stays the global default via IconContext.
- **Reusable `Switch` rebuilt** (`src/shared/components/ui/Switch.tsx`): cva track/thumb variants (sm/md), champagne-gradient checked state, recessed-steel unchecked; three layouts — bare (aria-label), inline label, settings row (label + description). Now used by the armory card public/private toggle (`PieceShareSwitch`), the feat form's visibility toggle, admin unassign's "also remove their records" purge option, and the existing settings panel.
- **Grid/Vault switcher shrunk** to a two-icon pill in the armory header (grid ⇄ vault), out of the layout's way; the old full-width tablist is gone.
- **Challenges became a bento card + categorized log**: the card face shows the nearest-to-done challenge with its progress bar and the forged count; clicking opens an overlay grouping every challenge under The Forge / The Ritual / The Record / The Honor (`ChallengeCategory` on every definition).
- **Passport share links now open the sharer's public armory** (`/armory/$handle`) instead of the shop page — first share enables sharing and mints the handle in one move (tab pre-opened inside the click gesture so popup blockers stay quiet); the share studio inherits the armory URL too. Passport tokens still never travel.

- **Phantom unassigned piece — fully self-healing**: `set_passport_visibility` failures pass the RPC's own error through, so a `not_owner` answer from *any* armory action (wear, visibility, Hall of Honor pin) now toasts and refetches the owned list immediately.

## 2026-07-17 — Feedback round 10: feats root-caused, pedestal armory, Phosphor trial

- **Feats not showing — root-caused**: the feats SELECT was missing `product_slug`, so every feat parsed with `productSlug: null` and the per-piece filters (grid cards, passport tab) matched nothing. Also armory reads (`listArmoryFeats`, `listOwnedPassports`) now **throw** on failure instead of returning `[]` — a swallowed error was being cached by React Query as a successful empty list.
- **Phantom unassigned piece self-heals**: a `not_owner` answer to any action now toasts "no longer registered to you" and refetches the armory so the piece drops immediately; the owned list also refetches on every mount (`staleTime: 0`) instead of serving the 30s-stale default.
- **Guest armory redesigned**: the athlete leads — emblem, name, rank, and a **record-numbers strip** (pieces / wears / feats); the **Hall of Honor stands on pedestals** (spotlight cone, floating piece with floor reflection, perspective plinth with an engraved I/II/III, light pool); every collection piece carries its wear count and **its own public feats**; feats on unshared pieces list under "More feats" with their piece name.
- **Google icon** (official multicolor G, inline SVG) on the social sign-in button.
- **Phosphor icon trial (reversible)**: added `@phosphor-icons/react` behind a new abstraction seam — `@/shared/icons` re-exports Phosphor under the historical lucide names (112 files repointed by codemod; `Anvil` stays lucide — Phosphor has none). Global weight is **duotone** via IconContext (`PHOSPHOR_ICON_WEIGHT` — one line to flip to regular/bold/fill). **lucide-react intentionally kept**: reverting is a one-file swap in the shim; final cleanup happens after the user's verdict.

## 2026-07-17 — Toast system redesigned: "the forged plate"

Complete replacement of the default sonner look (`AnvlToaster` + `.anvl-toast*` in `styles.css`); every existing `toast.*` call site renders through it unchanged.

- **Shape**: a beveled obsidian plate — two clipped corners (stamped-metal `clip-path`), 2px radius, brushed-metal top light (inset highlight), gradient surface from elevated-steel into near-black, and a deep `drop-shadow` that follows the bevel (box-shadow can't).
- **Heat bar**: a 3px type-colored edge on the left that **flares on arrival** and settles into a soft glow — champagne by default, success/warning/destructive tokens per type. The plate itself stays obsidian so all types read as one family.
- **Light sweep**: a one-time skewed highlight crosses the plate as it lands — metal catching the forge light.
- **Maker's mark**: the icon sits in a bordered, bevel-clipped square stamp cell, tinted by type; custom lucide icons (Check / Info / Flame for warning / TriangleAlert / spinning Loader2).
- **Layout**: Sora title in heading color, muted description, dismiss as a quiet corner control revealed on hover (sonner's default sat half-outside the plate and the bevel would clip it); action buttons restyled as forged champagne chips.
- **Position**: bottom-center rising from the forge; mobile offset clears the home indicator + sticky bars. Reduced motion drops flare + sweep. Verified live in the preview (computed styles: bevel, gradient, heat bar, sweep animation, stamp cell, repositioned close).

## 2026-07-17 — Feedback round 8: loud armory writes, OG unfurl fix

- **Silent write failures eliminated**: live-DB forensics showed feats + visibility writes DID land at 11:30 UTC (server healthy; PostgREST sees `admin_unassign_passport` — anon probe returns the expected 401), so the reported "not working" is browser-session rot being **swallowed by the client code**. Every armory write (feat create/edit/delete, per-piece visibility, wear, honor pin) now: (1) preflights the session and **force-refreshes a dying access token** before writing, and (2) returns the REAL failure reason, which the mutation **toasts** — nothing no-ops silently anymore. Feat forms only close on a confirmed success (a failed save keeps the form + values).
- **Shared links now unfurl as cards**: the site's `og:image` pointed at an SVG, which WhatsApp/Facebook/Telegram scrapers can't render — links degraded to plain text. Rasterized `og-default.svg` → **`og-default.png` (1200×630, 33 KB)** via sharp and pointed every share-image reference at it (in-app product-image fallback stays SVG). Social intents already carry the URL in their link field — with a scrapeable image the previews now render as proper link cards.

## 2026-07-17 — Feedback round 7: ceremony smoothness, claim confirm, passport share studio

- **Ceremony forge, smoothed**: the crest no longer waits for the product image — its silhouette samples **alone** and the entrance is a **disperse-in** (scattered embers assemble into the crest, Coming-Soon style); the product silhouette streams into the morph target in the background and is swapped only while the morph hasn't begun (no mid-flight pop, no stutter). The regrouped piece is now **sized off the live viewport** to match the DOM render (was a fixed world constant that overshot). Disperse eased `sine.inOut`.

- **Passport share = the full studio**: "Create a share image" on the passport now opens the same share modal as the Armory (formats, templates, gallery/**camera photo + HUD overlays**) preset to that piece — so the owner can post themselves wearing it. Modal gained `initialSubjectKey`.
- "View this product in the shop" → **"View in shop"**, smaller on mobile. Share-image icon → `Images` (lucide).

No overlapping morph mush. Perf: the passport/console **no longer mounts underneath the playing ceremony** (was double WebGL + context churn).

Mobile-first single column, internal scroll, no border.

## 2026-07-17 — Feedback round 3: ceremony v3, share studio, sheets, purge switch

- **Ceremony v3** (scan removed): the ANVL crest stands in embers for 0.5s → the embers disperse (burst mid-morph) → reassemble into the registered piece (sampled from the real product image) → the crisp render resolves with the seal + "Registered to <name>" and a **Continue to your Armory** button. Nothing auto-advances; a quiet "View this passport" link is the secondary exit. Verification lines + beam deleted (`ceremonyLines` lib removed).

New **image studio**: subject (your armory / any piece / any feat) × format (Story 1080×1920 / Post 1080×1350 / Message 1080×1080) × template (Forge / Champagne / Stealth) × backdrop (brand gradient or a **gallery/camera photo** with a legibility scrim) → download or native file share.

- **Topbar**: an **Armory** link appears in the desktop nav when signed in (hydration-safe mount gate), gone when signed out.

- **Unassign with purge switch** (`admin_unassign_passport` SECURITY DEFINER RPC, editor/admin verified server-side): the confirm dialog gains "Also remove their records for this product" — ON deletes the ex-owner's feats for the product (as if never owned); OFF frees the QR but keeps their feats, so re-claiming the same product reattaches records without duplicates.

## 2026-07-17 — Feedback round 2: share fix, round veil, synced forges, feats-in-cards, War Record

- **Share toggle actually works now**: `set_armory_share` was throwing at runtime — `gen_random_bytes` lives in the `extensions` schema, invisible under the function's `set search_path = public`, so the handle mint failed and the toggle silently did nothing. Re-minted with the built-in `gen_random_uuid()` (32 hex chars, 122 bits); migration `20260717130000`, verified live as the real user (rolled back).
- **Rounder disperse**: the section-swap veil is now an **elliptical** cloud centred on the cards' region — sqrt-sampled density falling toward a feathered rim (dimmer shades at the edge) instead of a uniform box, so the burst reads as a soft bloom, not a square of static.
- **Performance**: console forge 9k → 6k particles, canvas dpr capped 1.75 → 1.5 (additive point sprites gain nothing above it).
- **Synced load forges**: the product render's first-load forge now runs on the SAME `passportForgeTiming` clock as the bento cards' entry — piece and cards gather and resolve as one.
- **Feats live in the piece's card**: the standalone Feats section is gone; each Grid card embeds `PieceFeats` (add/edit/delete inline, date + public switch, product pre-assigned — no picker).
- **Guest War Record**: `/armory/$handle` gets a designed card — watermark crest, owner name, stats row (pieces forged / wears logged / feats), and every public feat with its piece chip + date. Route resolves catalog names so feats on non-public pieces still show their product.

## 2026-07-17 — Armory/passport feedback round (post-G live testing)

Fixes and refinements from the user's first real walkthrough of Phase G:

- **CSP**: `img-src` allows `https://*.googleusercontent.com` — Google OAuth avatars were blocked until a relog.
- **Pristine unassign**: admin unassign now wipes wear count, last-worn, Hall-of-Honor pin, visibility and any pending transfer alongside the claim, so the piece vanishes from the ex-owner's armory as if never owned.
- **Wear anti-spam**: `log_passport_wear` enforces one wear per 24h (undo exempt); `WoreItButton` shows a disabled "Worn today" state and the optimistic bump rolls back on rejection. Migration `20260717120000_armory_life_refinements` (applied + smoke-tested).
- **Feats by product**: `armory_feats.product_slug` + a "Wearing" piece picker in the form; the piece shows on the feat row and in the public armory.
- **Share sheet** (`ArmoryShareModal`): copy link · native share on mobile (all installed apps incl. Instagram/TikTok/Discord) · web intents for WhatsApp/Facebook/X/Telegram/Reddit · a generated 1080×1920 Instagram-story image (brand gradient, rank emblem, name, piece count, link) with download + native file share.
- **Rank ladder modal**: the rank card opens every rank (levels I–III + unlock conditions, current highlighted, future locked) and the badge catalogue with earned ones marked.
- **Compact Hall of Honor**: single slim row of thumbnails instead of the 3-pedestal grid.
- **Animation perf**: ceremony 5.5s→~3.0s, console assembly 2.0s→1.1s, first-load product forge 2.6s→1.3s (6k→4k points); section swaps disperse into a veil **bounded to the cards' own region** (`buildLocalVeil`) and recollect fast (out 0.42s / hold 0.08s / in 0.72s) — continuous, no stall, never the whole page.
- **Layout**: every product render in a fixed 4:5 box (42vh, max 26rem — same size, smaller); bento top-aligned under the tabs; mobile render in a fixed 4:5 box with the forge intro desktop-only (no extra WebGL context on phones).
- **Console noise**: GSAP "target not found" on the mobile passport guarded (empty-selector states); tall modals scroll (`max-h-[85svh]`).

## 2026-07-16 — Passport Phase G: the Armory comes alive (G1–G7)

Approved from the Phase G menu: wear journal, Feats, Hall of Honor, public armory (both directions), verified-owner reviews, full gamification. Declined/deferred: lifecycle (care reminders, repair log, retirement) and owner perks (early access, exclusives). Shipped as G1–G7, one commit each; final `pnpm verify` green (130 files / 722 tests), `vendor-three` stays lazy.

- **G1 — DB foundation** (`20260716120000_armory_life.sql`): `product_passports` gains `wear_count`/`last_worn_at` + `featured_slot` (1-3, unique per owner); new `armory_feats` (per-entry public flag) + `product_reviews` (one per owner/product); `storefront_profiles` gains `armory_public` + a minted-once `armory_handle` (72 bits). Every anon read is a SECURITY DEFINER RPC projecting only safe fields — never tokens, serials, ids or emails. Smoke-tested.
- **G2 — wear journal + Feats**: one-tap optimistic "Wore it" counter per Grid piece (muted undo); Feats = add/edit/delete achievement log with date + per-entry public/private switch.
- **G3 — Hall of Honor**: star-pin up to three pieces (disabled when full, never silent eviction); shrine pedestal strip; `honorSlots.ts` pure + tested.
- **G4 — public armory (two states)**: `ArmoryShareCard` (owner, read+write) mints a shareable link; `/armory/$handle` (`PublicArmoryView`, read-only) shows name, rank, Hall of Honor, shared pieces + public feats — no controls, pieces aren't links. noindex + Coming-Soon-exempt.
- **G5 — verified-owner reviews**: `PdpReviews` — everyone reads (Verified owner badge + average); only a passport-holding owner of that product sees the write form (proven by `submit_product_review`). One review per owner, edit + delete.
- **G6 — gamification** (all client-derived, same tamper posture as ranks): Forge XP/Level on a quadratic curve (`forgeXp.ts`, `ForgeProgress`) with the nearest goal always dangled; an evolving Collection Crest SVG (rivets/laurel/crown/star/glow by milestone); a Challenges quest log (`challenges.ts`, `ArmoryChallenges`), nearest-to-done first. Logic pure + tested.
- **G7 — particle polish**: bento ember tracing now walks a TRUE rounded-rect outline (four edges + four quarter-circle corners, `pointOnRoundedRect`) so corners curve like `rounded-2xl`; edge jitter tightened ±3→±1.4px. The passport product render forges from embers sampled off the real image on first load only (`ProductForgeImage` → lazy canvas), then unmounts; reduced motion / no WebGL shows the image immediately.

## 2026-07-15 — Passport Phase F: related products ("Complete the Loadout")

Approved: **two** LEGACY-tab sections, owner-only, deliberately subtle (they mirror the collection gap already visible in the Armory and link to the shop PDP — never a popup or a checkout push; never shown on the public authenticity view).

- **Complete the drop** — the rest of this piece's drop the owner hasn't registered yet; once they hold the whole drop it becomes a quiet **completion seal** (crest + "<drop> — complete") rather than an empty strip.
- **Matching pieces** — other pieces in the same commerce category still to collect; a one-line acknowledgement when they hold them all.
- `lib/relatedProducts.ts` (pure, 9 tests) builds the candidate lists in the SSR loader (user-independent); the strip filters by the owner's registrations **client-side** via `useOwnedPassportsQuery` (SSR is anon — the same split as the size recommendation). Gated on `view.isOwner`, so it never appears for someone else scanning the code.
- **Verified**: `pnpm verify` green (124 files / 695 tests, incl. new related-products + strip tests).

## 2026-07-15 — Passport Phase E: the registration ceremony, "The Authentication"

Approved concept, and approved that the verification beat carries **real record data**. The ceremony plays only after the atomic claim has already succeeded — it never gates registration.

- **Beat sheet** on a shared clock (`webgl/ceremonyTiming.ts`; the DOM timeline and the particle forge never call each other — both schedule against the same constants, so the hand-off can't desync): champagne beam sweeps down and a lit copy wipes in behind it via `clip-path`, so the beam genuinely *reveals* the piece out of the dark (0.35s) → real record lines tick in (1.35s) → embers gather into the ANVL crest (2.35s) → **the seal locks as the embers fuse into it** with a champagne flash (3.25s) → "Registered to <name>" settles (3.85s) → "Added to your Armory" (4.5s) → hand-off.
- **The crest is the real mark** (`CeremonyCrestParticles.tsx`): embers sample `/brand/mark.svg` through `sampleImageSilhouette` and reuse the passport forge shaders, so the cloud *is* the crest and the solid seal resolves **out of** it (`uReveal` fusion) rather than crossfading over it — the particle-forge standard, not a lookalike.
- **Honesty rule** (`lib/ceremonyLines.ts`, 4 tests): every line is a true statement from the record just written (Piece · Drop · Material · Origin · Colorway · Size · Registered); anything without a real value is **omitted, never faked** — no "AUTHENTICATING…" theater implying checks that aren't happening, and an unknown origin key produces no line rather than a bogus one. No serial engraving (final product decision).
- Mobile-first: DOM/CSS/GSAP core with the crest forge + ember field gated behind `isWebglAvailable()` + the canvas teardown guard; reduced motion skips straight to the passport.
- **Verified**: `pnpm verify` green (122 files / 686 tests); `vendor-three` stays lazy.

## 2026-07-15 — Passport Phase D: Armory views (Vault · Collection · Timeline · Loadout)

Approved this session: Vault, Collection, Timeline, Loadout — Grid stays the default. Declined/deferred: **Hall of Honor** (needs a `featured` flag per passport; deferred to the personalization family so its storage is designed once), **Mannequin** (needs a rigged 3D garment per product — only the anvil/hammer GLBs exist, so it would have been a fabrication), **Archive** (meaningless until lifecycle/retirement exists).

- **Shaping is pure and tested** (`passport/lib/armory.ts`, 10 tests): `buildVaultDrops`, `buildCollectionDrops`, `buildTimeline`, `buildLoadout`. The views (`account/panels/armory/ArmoryViews.tsx`) are presentational, CSS-only motion, `motion-safe:` gated.
- **Vault** — your *started* drops as a lit slot wall: registered pieces are champagne-washed plates that lift on hover, the rest of the drop sits as dashed empty sockets so the gap is visible. It never lists untouched drops (an armory is a record of what you own, not a shop shelf).
- **Collection** — every drop with a completion bar; owned pieces as champagne chips linking to their passport, missing ones as dashed chips.
- **Timeline** — every registered unit newest-first on a champagne spine, with drop, colorway/size and date.
- **Loadout** — grouped by the product's commerce `category`; anything without one lands in an `Other` catch-all (sorted last) rather than being invented into a slot.
- Semantics worth knowing: duplicate units of the same product collapse into **one** Vault/Collection slot (newest fills it) but keep **separate** Timeline rows — a slot is a product, a timeline row is a unit. The Armory catalog query now also carries name/image/category.
- **Verified**: `pnpm verify` green (121 files / 682 tests, incl. 14 new Armory tests).

## 2026-07-15 — Passport Phase C: design-detail hotspots (pulse markers + side reveal, click-to-place authoring) + console fit fixes

- **Design-detail hotspots** (approved concept: pulse markers + side reveal). Champagne markers pulse on the product render; selecting one dims the rest, stops the pulse, and reveals its copy — in the console it takes the panel beside the piece (**the embers re-trace around the new card**, since the measure effect already follows the layout), on mobile it's a sheet under the render. The overlay is pointer-transparent except the markers, so the render keeps its sheen and never steals clicks.
- **Click-to-place authoring** (approved): the wizard's new "Design details" step shows the hero render — click to drop a numbered marker, then write its title/story. Positions are stored as **percent of the image box**, so markers hold their spot at any display size; the resolver clamps defensively so a bad value can't park a marker off-image, and untitled hotspots are dropped. The step explains itself when no hero render is assigned yet. No migration (jsonb + `.catch()` defaults + back-fill).
- **Console fit fixes** (reported): both columns are now `flex` + `min-h-0`, so the render and the panel **shrink** to the leftover height instead of spilling past the bottom on short laptops — the console never scrolls, and the grid gained real bottom padding. The **world map is capped tighter** (22vh, narrower figure, compact legend) so the origin section fits its panel rather than scrolling.
- **Verified**: `pnpm verify` green (119 files / 668 tests, incl. 4 new hotspot tests covering percent positioning, select/dismiss, and the empty case).

## 2026-07-15 — Passport Phase B: specifications, interactive care guide, fit & sizing with size recommendations, forge notes

- **Specifications panel** (CRAFT): construction, fit type, compression, stretch, breathability, intended use — unauthored rows hide rather than render empty.
- **Interactive care guide** (RITUAL): `careSymbols.tsx` draws 13 standard care marks as inline SVG in brand tokens (wash temps, bleach/tumble/iron/dry-clean prohibitions, inside-out, line/flat dry); tapping a mark explains it in plain language, and each numbered step expands to the CMS-authored "why" note. CSS/DOM only — works on every device, `motion-safe:` gated.
- **Fit & sizing** (RITUAL) + **personal size recommendation** (approved this session): every product maps its own sizes to a **canonical body size** (`fit.sizeEquivalence` — an oversized cut's `M → S`), so a registered size translates across the armory: this size → canonical → the other product's size. `buildPassportSizeGuide()` runs in the SSR loader (user-independent); `recommendSizes()` applies the viewer's size client-side, since SSR is anon and only the owner ever learns their registered size. **It stays silent rather than guessing** (no map / unmapped size / no matching canonical → no advice), and the map is CMS-maintained per product so a cut that runs big is mapped honestly. 11 unit tests cover the translation, case-insensitivity, and every silent path.
- **Forge notes** (LEGACY): expandable `Title|Body` development fact cards.
- **Wizard grew to 9 steps** (specs, care symbols + step notes, fit + size map, forge notes) — `passport_content` is jsonb with `.catch()` defaults and back-filling, so **no migration was needed**.
- **Verified**: `pnpm verify` green (118 files / 664 tests).

## 2026-07-15 — Passport Phase A: no serials, owner visibility switch, ember bento cards, group tabs, in-page story, world map, rank levels

Implements the locked ANVL Passport product decisions + the UX corrections. **Ask-before-build protocol is now in force** for every optional feature (each phase opens with its own yes/no questions).

- **Serial numbers removed from every customer surface** (final decision): new `AuthenticityPlate` (ANVL crest + "Authentic ANVL" + drop + "Limited to N pieces") replaces `ForgeSerialPlate` on the teaser, onboarding, ceremony seal (crest stamps in — no serial engraving), console, dossier, Armory cards **and the printed QR card**. `serial_number` remains internal (admin ledger/ordering); no UI formatter is exported.
- **Owner-controlled passport visibility** (migration `20260715120000_passport_visibility.sql`): `is_public` (default **false**) + owner-only `set_passport_visibility` RPC. `get_passport_by_token` now branches — a claimed piece shows its engraved name/date to non-owners only when the owner opted in (or when the caller holds a live transfer code); otherwise an anonymous "already registered" verification. Switch lives beside the plate on the owner's passport.
- **Particles moved off the product onto the bento cards**: the ember layer now traces the **measured card rects** (borders + sparse fill) rather than the product silhouette — so they settle exactly where the cards are. Accuracy rules baked in: panel content fades only (never transforms — a moving rect desyncs the trace), rects are read the frame after commit with change-detection, and `forgeTo` now **freezes the cloud's current interpolated position** (CPU mirror of the shader's per-seed stagger) so an interrupted morph continues from where the embers actually are instead of snapping back. Cards materialize as the embers settle; the product render is clean with a champagne `.pp-sheen` sweep.
- **Group tabs above the bento** — THE CRAFT · THE RITUAL · THE LEGACY (`PASSPORT_SECTIONS` gained `group`); icons replaced by champagne numerals; the mobile dossier renders the same groups as headings.
- **Story opens in-page** (`PassportStoryChapter` — acts stacked with `StoryMedia`); the loader returns the full `StoryChapter` instead of a boolean; the redirect card is gone.
- **Origin → world map with two CMS pins** (`WorldOriginMap` + `passportCountries.ts`, 37 presets, equirectangular projection): **Designed in** (outline) and **Made in** (pulsing) joined by a champagne arc, both picked in the wizard's origin step (`origin.madeIn/designedIn`). Height-capped so it fits — detail panels scroll only when content genuinely exceeds them.
- **Footer hidden on `/p/*`**; **Coming Soon lightened** (backdrop 40%→55%, vignette 62/88%→40/72%, legibility shield 74/48%→55/32%); mobile passport hero image deliberately small.
- **Ranks: three levels each** (Initiate I–III · Forged I–III · Oathbound I–III · Warlord I–III, level pips in the Armory) with **four Higgsfield-generated emblems** (obsidian + champagne heraldry, escalating ornateness) cut out, downscaled to 256px (28–65 KB) into `public/brand/ranks/`. Serial-based "Early Steel" badge removed.
- **Verified**: `pnpm verify` green (116 files / 650 tests); migration applied + RPC privacy smoke-tested (anon private → no name; public → name); advisors show only the intentional RPC exposures; test rows cleaned up.

## 2026-07-14 — Passport console fixed: measured particle registration, soft veil transitions, bulletproof section swap

- **Particles matched to the render.** The ember silhouette floated larger than the product because the form was normalized to a fixed world size while the DOM image was `max-h-[58vh]` — no registration. The console now **measures the rendered image rect** (ResizeObserver + settle re-measures → `motion.stage`) and the particle system lerps its **position and scale** to it every frame, so embers and render match 1:1 at any layout/viewport.
- **Shatter → soft veil.** The section transition was too aggressive: burst 0.9→0.35, morphs lengthened (out 0.8s `sine.inOut`, reform 1.4s `power2.inOut`), mid-morph puff 0.3→0.14, shimmer and point-size cap reduced, scatter cloud shallower — the composition now *dissolves and breathes back* instead of exploding.
- **Section content could vanish** (the reported "content nowhere to be found"): the panel swap hinged on a mid-timeline GSAP callback and competing reveal tweens. Rebuilt: the swap is **React state + `setTimeout` + CSS wrapper transitions**; GSAP staggers are decoration on top; `motion.reveal` has a single owner per moment (`killTweensOf` before every writer); WebGL capability is resolved synchronously so the entrance clock is right from frame one. New jsdom regression test (`passportConsole.test.tsx`) drives open → back → mid-flight re-click with **no animation clock at all**.
- **Verified**: `pnpm verify` green (116 files / 647 tests).

## 2026-07-14 — Passports phase 2: particle console, ownership transfer, content wizard, forced asset naming (branch `feature/product-passport`)

- **Passport console (desktop redesign).** Owners on ≥1280px motion-capable screens now get a **no-scroll** passport: the piece forged out of ~11k ember particles on the left stage (new `src/features/passport/webgl/` — `passportForgeShaders/Timing/MotionState`, `PassportForgeParticles/Canvas/Gate`, silhouette sampled from the CMS hero render via the shared `sampleImageSilhouette`, ANVL-mark fallback, DustField layered in), bento section cards on the right. **Opening a section shatters the whole composition** — particles morph to a screen-filling cloud while the DOM blurs/scatters — and re-forges it around the section detail (origin map, material dossier, care ritual, details, authenticity); back reverses. DOM choreography stands alone without WebGL; phones/tablets/reduced-motion keep the scrolling dossier. Both surfaces render from one `PASSPORT_SECTIONS` registry so they can't drift.
- **Claim onboarding rebuilt** — product render panel (colorway-aware) + serial plate over a shared `PassportAtmosphere` backdrop (blurred product wash + ember glow, replaces the flat black), staggered GSAP entrance; the teaser got the same atmosphere.

Storefront: "Transfer ownership" modal on the owner passport (mint/copy/cancel link `/p/<token>?transfer=<code>`), recipient accept screen with name-on-plate → the claim ceremony replays; sign-in redirect preserves the code. Stage machine + tests extended (`transfer_offer`/`transfer_teaser`).
- **CMS passport content wizard.** Migration `20260714121000_passport_content.sql` adds `passport_content` jsonb to both singletons (pdp_content pattern). New `passportContent.zod/.settings` (localStorage working copy, `adminCmsRemoteSync` field, tolerant admin hydration). `/admin/passports` is now **two tabs**: *QR codes* (existing generator/ledger, layout polish) and *Passport content* — authored-products list + a **multi-step wizard** (one step per passport section: identity, piece, material, care, details & story, origin — each with its own copy and `MediaLibrarySlotField` assets; the piece step's transparent hero render feeds the particle silhouette). Storefront `resolvePassportContent()` layers passport_content → pdp_content → product fields → code defaults (tested).

- **The particle-forge standard documented** as the house quality bar — full recipe (fixed pool, silhouette sampling, per-seed stagger, uniform vocabulary, shared choreography clock, motion-state bridge, gates/fallbacks, feel targets) in `docs/animation-guidelines.md` + a pointer in CLAUDE.md; the passport forge is built on it.
- **Verified**: `pnpm verify` green (typecheck clean; 115 files / 645 tests incl. new transfer-stage + resolver suites; build clean — passport webgl chunks stay lazy).

## 2026-07-14 — Product Passports ("The Forge Ledger"): per-unit QR claim flow, Armory account tab, admin generator (branch `feature/product-passport`)

- **New Supabase table `product_passports`** (`supabase/migrations/20260713120000_product_passports.sql`, applied via MCP): one row per physical unit — secret `token` (the QR encodes `/p/<token>`), `batch_id`, product slug/name snapshot, `serial_number`/`edition_total` forge plates, and claim snapshots (`claimed_by/at/color/size/email/display_name`). **No public SELECT** (tokens would be enumerable via PostgREST); anon/owner reads go through the `get_passport_by_token` SECURITY DEFINER RPC (safe projection; owner-only fields gated on `auth.uid()`), and claiming is the `claim_passport` RPC — an atomic `UPDATE … WHERE token = $1 AND claimed_by IS NULL`, so exactly one owner ever, even under concurrent scans. Owner RLS select powers the Armory; CMS editor/admin policies cover generate/unassign/delete. Fixed during verification: `is_owner` used `claimed_by = auth.uid()` bare, which is SQL NULL (not false) for anon callers and failed the Zod boolean parse — now `coalesce(…, false)`.
- **Storefront `/p/$token`** (`src/routes/p/$token.tsx` + `src/features/passport/`): SSR loader does the anon RPC via a new `restRpc` helper (`src/features/cms/api/supabaseRest.ts` — no supabase-js in the loader path) + product/`resolvePdpContent`/story-chapter fetches; `noindex`. Client state machine (`resolvePassportStage`, pure + unit-tested): unknown token → branded "not of the forge"; unclaimed + signed out → teaser with `/auth/sign-in?redirect=/p/<token>` (existing `sanitizeInternalRedirect` flow); unclaimed + signed in → onboarding (self-reported colorway/size via `ColorSwatch`/`SizeSelector`, engraved display name seeded from the profile) → claim RPC → **claim ceremony** (full-screen GSAP timeline: strike flash, stage shake, 26 DOM spark particles, plate stamp, name engrave — mobile-first, no pinning; lazy `vendor-three` `DustField` ember layer behind it on `isWebglAvailable()` devices with the canvas teardown-guard pattern; reduced motion skips straight to the passport) → owner dossier (identity plate, colorway imagery, material dossier, care ritual, design details/story, story-chapter CTA, "Forged in Lebanon" SVG origin map, authenticity footer). Claimed + anyone else → public authenticity view ("Forged by <name>", claim date, verified badge). Page motion: GSAP entrance everywhere, `≥768px` adds batched ScrollTrigger reveals.
- **Armory account tab**: `ACCOUNT_TABS` gains `armory` (5th panel, Shield icon) → `ArmoryPanel` — claimed pieces as bento cards with serial plates + passport links, drop-completion progress bars, and client-derived ranks (Initiate → Forged → Oathbound → Warlord) + badges (First Strike, Early Steel ≤ #10, Drop Complete) from `src/features/passport/lib/ranks.ts`.
- **Admin `/admin/passports`** (nav badge "QR"): Story-editor pattern (direct table CRUD on the admin browser client, `Result<T>` unions, Zod at the write boundary) in `src/features/admin/passports/`. Generate batches (product picker from `useAdminProductCatalogQuery`, manual quantity 1–500 — Shopify Storefront API exposes no stock counts; serials continue from the product max, new rows carry the new `edition_total` while old rows keep their historic denominator — unit-tested), claimed/unclaimed ledger with claimant email/name snapshots + filters + stats rail, unassign/delete/delete-batch behind `AdminConfirmDialog`, copy-claim-URL, and a lazy **print QR sheet** (`PassportPrintSheet` + new `qrcode` dep, admin-only chunk) with print-isolation CSS → browser "Save as PDF".
- **Coming Soon gate**: `/p` and `/auth` added to `COMING_SOON_EXEMPT_PREFIXES` — QR cards ship with physical products, so passports (and their sign-in step) stay reachable in reveal mode; gate tests extended.
- Docs: new `docs/features/product-passport.md`; CLAUDE.md (features/routes/schema/admin tables), `docs/backend-guidelines.md` policy model, `docs/cms-architecture.md` admin table.
- **Verified**: `pnpm verify` green (typecheck clean; 114 files / 639 tests incl. 28 new passport/ranks/stage/batch tests + extended gate tests; build clean — `PassportPrintSheet`/`qrcode` and the ceremony ember layer confirmed as separate lazy chunks). Live walk on `pnpm dev` + real Supabase: seeded test rows → teaser (serial plate + redirect links verified), public "Forged by" view, branded not-found; RPC smoke tests (`not_authenticated`, `already_claimed` paths); Supabase security advisors show only the two intentional RPC-exposure notices; test rows cleaned up.

## 2026-07-12 — Media library: multiselect, rename, GLB previews, assignment/format filters; Coming Soon flash fixed for real

- **Admin `/admin/assets` media library gains bulk actions, rename, and richer filtering.** `MediaAssetGrid` now has per-card checkboxes + a "Select all" toolbar with **Download selected** (sequential blob-downloads) and **Delete selected** (new `deleteMediaAssets` service fn + `bulkDeleteMutation`, confirm dialog reports per-asset failures). Filenames are now **editable inline** (mirrors the existing alt-text pattern; new `updateMediaAssetFilename` service fn + `renameMutation`) — the underlying storage object is untouched, only the catalog row's `filename`.
